- **decision-journal**: Use when making a non-trivial decision (hire/fire, big bet, strategic pivot, large purchase, deal terms). Reads STATE.md &lt;decisions&gt;, pipes every line through scripts/lib/pseudonymize.cjs (git identity + stable hash) so author names are masked before leaving the repo, and writes a read-only page via the connections/notion.md interface on cycle close. Write-only (non-GDD users read; they never write back). Degrades to a local markdown file when no Notion connection is available.
